- plugin `rosw` v0.1.0 (`.claude-plugin/plugin.json`): The ROSW pipelines: a checkpointed creative-writing pipeline with its vault, chunk tagging and plot-logic checks, the studio pipeline that walks one made thing through six stages and records it in the
- `.claude/agents/brush-librarian-native.md`: Native (Claude-only, no script) condenser for the brush vault, the counterpart of creative-writing-librarian-native. Given brush ids with their vault-relative note path and line range as brush_search.py reports them, and an optional focus, it reads exactly tho
- `.claude/agents/brush-tagger.md`: Tags ONE batch of brushes (the contact sheets that brush_tags.py plan names for one call) from the closed vocabulary in brushes/vault/_BrushTags/vocabulary.yaml on six axes (medium, mark, texture, edge, motif, use), plus a one-sentence visual description, by l
- `.claude/agents/creative-writing-chunk-tagger.md`: Tags ONE vault work's chunks with plot_tags/context_tags/mood_tags/motif_tags from the closed vocabulary in creative-writing/vault/_ChunkTags/vocabulary.yaml, so creative-writing/vault/tools/vault_search.py can surface per-chunk (not just per-file) matches. In
- `.claude/agents/creative-writing-drafter.md`: Generates the heavy-lift artifact for one stage of the creative-writing pipeline (outline, draft, or self-revision) from context the caller provides. Use only for these three stages -- not intake or reference_pull (data-gathering, not generation) and never for
- `.claude/agents/creative-writing-librarian-native.md`: Native (Claude-only, no-Ollama) equivalent of creative-writing/pipeline/librarian.py's condense_file/digest_files for the creative-writing-pipeline skill. Reads exactly the vault files it's given and returns a condensed Markdown digest per file, in the SAME fo
- `.claude/skills/blender-gemini/SKILL.md`: Drive the author's open Blender through the Gemini CLI by calling the gemini MCP server (ask-gemini), which has the Blender MCP server registered. - `.claude/skills/brush-designer/SKILL.md`: Runs the brush designer app for the author and helps them change or build a Procreate brush in it. - `.claude/skills/brush-library/SKILL.md`: Answers questions about the author's Procreate brush library (their brush packs and Procreate's own legacy sets) from the brush vault, by searching it with brushes/vault/tools/brush_search.py or the brushes MCP tools and citing brush ids, sets and note lines.

- `.claude/skills/chunk-tag-backfill/SKILL.md`: Runs (or re-runs) the creative-writing-chunk-tagger subagent over the vault's work files, writing/updating each one's creative-writing/vault/_ChunkTags/ sidecar. Use when backfilling per-chunk tags across the vault, or re-tagging specific files after an edit. 
- `.claude/skills/creative-writing-pipeline/SKILL.md`: Walk a new idea for this vault through the checkpointed multi-stage pipeline (intake, reference pull, outline, draft, self-revision, vault integration) instead of writing a finished piece in one shot. - `.claude/skills/higgsfield-library-sync/SKILL.md`: Import the author's Higgsfield generations (images, videos, audio, 3D models) into the studio's generations library at studio/library/, or bring it up to date. - `.claude/skills/reach-references/SKILL.md`: Gather outside reference material for a studio piece - pages, threads, videos and articles that show what a category looks like or how people talk about it - as a cited reference note the author can keep with a studio run. Each file below is quoted from the repository at HEAD. Lines start with ">" so its headings do not mix with this document's.

### Source file: README.md

> # ROSW
>
> **New here?** Open [`docs/index.html`](docs/index.html) for a page per tool, and run
> `python docs/setup_keys.py` to add any API keys (most of ROSW needs none).
>
> One repo, multiple tools. Each tool gets a self-contained folder. The
> creative-writing tool is used from this repo, so its skills and agents live once
> at the repo root under `.claude/` and load whenever this repo is the working
> directory. The studio tool is used from this repo too, and keeps its skill
> there. The ui-design tool is a Claude Code plugin published on its own, so
> its skills and agents live inside it, under `plugins/ui-design/`; the other
> plugins under `plugins/` are built the same way. The repo root is a plugin too
> (`rosw`), which carries the `.claude/` skills and agents; they work only from a
> clone of this repo.
>
> ```
